Modelling choices are provisional: RR weakens closed-source log odds by fraction x; RT discounts the same log odds with exponential fading and saved independent tag retrieval after record 48. W receives a noisy harmful-intent diagnostic before observations. IG pools a common latent agenda over independent source truths; outsider library has strength 0.5, insider library includes 1 with honesty prior 0.5+b. This library/prior contrast is not a human mechanism. Partial tag loss is compared with its no-switch counterfactual.

The packet lists some finite-sample expectations as by construction. Those rows remain scale-only; their empirical bar outcomes are not logical guarantees.

Case: excluded case check passed.

Complete raw replay and independent reconstruction passed. Shared generators and some numerical kernels remain a limitation. Intervals are pointwise descriptive and are not multiplicity-adjusted; adaptive follow-ups do not convert them to confirmation.

Sounding Line: no human test this week.
ToMpathy: RR/RT scale comparisons do not license a retroactive tag; working position C-1107-17 remains untested by this miniature.

Earlier-record dispositions (each identifier is a separate record):

- `G-V26-DAY-G3-1`: Potentially more informative conditional reading: K5 exposed the role of exactly balanced evidence at closure. K5-R1 is a separately frozen level-one retained-data reread with closure balance as a covariate; it reproduces the earlier scores before stratification and overwrites nothing.
- `G-V26-DAY-G3-T09-1`: No changed reading: the expanded source-library result remains intact. K5 changes group pooling and priors as well, so its group contrast is not an isolated test of the earlier library effect. No claim is reversed.
- `C-1107-17`: Curator position, not a previous empirical result. Its recorded status and wording remain unchanged; this constructed comparison does not establish a human mechanism.
